Manufacturing engineers often write defect reasons with typos, abbreviations, mixed Chinese-English text, and informal notation.\n\n## Key Challenges\n1. **Mixed Languages**: Chinese-English code-switching (e.g., \"R152_NET open，疑似虚焊\")\n2. **Typos/Variants**: Common misspellings (e.g., \"短露\" vs \"短路\", \"接處\" vs \"接触\")\n3. **Abbreviations**: Technical abbreviations (e.g., \"OS\" for open, \"NG\" for no good)\n4. **Multiple Defects**: A single text may describe multiple issues separated by punctuation\n5. **Noise Words**: Informal notes like \"已处理\", \"OK了\", \"重测过\", \"有问题\"\n\n## Text Segmentation Strategy\nRaw text often contains multiple defect mentions separated by:\n- Punctuation: `/`, `;`, `、`, `&`, `+`\n- Keywords: \"and\", \"同时\"\n- Line breaks or logical separators\n\n### Segmentation Algorithm\n```python\nimport re\n\ndef segment_raw_text(raw_text):\n    # Split on common separators\n    separators = r'[/;、&+]|\\band\\b|同时'\n    segments = re.split(separators, raw_text)\n    # Clean and filter empty segments\n    segments = [s.strip() for s in segments if s.strip()]\n    return segments\n```\n\n## Keyword Matching Strategy\nUse a weighted keyword matching approach:\n\n### Category Keywords\n| Category | Keywords (EN) | Keywords (CN) |\n|----------|--------------|---------------|\n| OPEN | open, OS | 开路 |\n| SHORT | short, bridge | 短路, 连锡, 桥连 |\n| COLD_SOLDER | cold solder, intermittent | 虚焊, 浮高 |\n| FIXTURE_CONTACT | fixture, pogo, reseat | 治具, 探针, 接触不良 |\n| FLASH_FAIL | flash fail, program fail | 烧录失败, 写入失败 |\n| CHECKSUM_FAIL | CRC, checksum, verify | 校验失败 |\n| LEAKAGE | hipot, leakage | 漏电, 绝缘不良 |\n| CURRENT_HIGH | current high, ICC | 功耗高, 静态电流 |\n| MISSING | missing | 少件 |\n| REVERSED | reversed, polarity | 反向 |\n| ESD | ESD | 静电 |\n| PROBE_WORN | probe worn | 探针磨损 |\n\n### Component Reference Extraction\n```python\nCOMPONENT_PATTERN = r'\\b(R\\d+|C\\d+|U\\d+|L\\d+|D\\d+|Q\\d+|J\\d+|T\\d+|Y\\d+|TP\\d+|ANT\\d+|SH\\d+)\\b'\nNET_PATTERN = r'\\b([A-Z0-9_]+(?:_NET|_SDA|_SCL|_VDD|_GND|_MOSI|_MISO|_TCK|_NODE|_FEED|_OUT|_IN))\\b'\n```\n\n## Confidence Calculation\n\n### Base Confidence Factors\n- Exact keyword match: +0.3\n- Component match: +0.2\n- Net/signal match: +0.15\n- Category alignment with station: +0.1\n- Chinese keyword match: +0.1\n- English keyword match: +0.1\n\n### Confidence Thresholds\n- **High (≥0.8)**: Strong match with multiple keyword matches\n- **Medium (0.5-0.8)**: Partial match, component/signal alignment\n- **Low (<0.5)**: Set pred_code=\"UNKNOWN\" for engineering review\n\n## Implementation Pattern\n\n```python\ndef calculate_match_score(segment, codebook_entry):\n    score = 0.0\n    keywords = codebook_entry['keywords_examples'].lower().split(',')\n    segment_lower = segment.lower()\n\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw = kw.strip()\n        if kw in segment_lower:\n            score += 0.15\n\n    # Check component match\n    if codebook_entry.get('component') and codebook_entry['component'] in segment:\n        score += 0.2\n\n    return min(score, 1.0)\n```\n\n## Handling Unknown Cases\nWhen no good match is found:\n- Set `pred_code = \"UNKNOWN\"`\n- Set `pred_label = \"\"`\n- Set `confidence < 0.5` (typically 0.2-0.4)\n- Provide rationale explaining why match failed\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":295,"cache_read_input_tokens":43041,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":295,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9ce674d7-fa98-40dd-bdc1-6dee18438441","uuid":"ef3ec6ff-f8a1-4645-ab60-ee523160dde1"}

but not break phrases\n\n    # First, handle the bilingual slash separators and semicolons\n    separators = r'(?:\\s*/\\s*|\\s*;\\s*|\\s*、\\s*|\\s*&\\s*|\\s*\\+\\s*)'\n\n    raw_segments = re.split(separators, raw_text)\n\n    segments = []\n    for seg in raw_segments:\n        seg = seg.strip()\n        if seg and len(seg) > 2:\n            segments.append(seg)\n\n    # Deduplicate similar segments (common in the data)\n    unique_segments = []\n    seen_normalized = set()\n    for seg in segments:\n        normalized = clean_for_matching(seg).lower()\n        # Skip if we've seen something very similar\n        is_dup = False\n        for seen in seen_normalized:\n            if len(normalized) > 5 and (normalized in seen or seen in normalized):\n                is_dup = True\n                break\n        if not is_dup and normalized:\n            unique_segments.append(seg)\n            seen_normalized.add(normalized)\n\n    return unique_segments if unique_segments else [raw_text]\n\n\ndef extract_components(text):\n    \"\"\"Extract component references from text.\"\"\"\n    return COMPONENT_PATTERN.findall(text)\n\n\ndef extract_signals(text):\n    \"\"\"Extract signal/net references from text.\"\"\"\n    return SIGNAL_PATTERN.findall(text)\n\n\ndef detect_category(text):\n    \"\"\"Detect the defect category from text.\"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    text_fixed = fix_typos(text)\n    text_fixed_lower = text_fixed.lower()\n\n    scores = {}\n    for category, info in CATEGORY_KEYWORDS.items():\n        score = 0\n        matched_keywords = []\n        for kw in info['primary']:\n            kw_lower = kw.lower()\n            if kw_lower in text_lower or kw_lower in text_fixed_lower:\n                score += 1\n                matched_keywords.append(kw)\n        scores[category] = (score, matched_keywords)\n\n    # Get best category\n    best_category = max(scores.keys(), key=lambda k: scores[k][0])\n    best_score, matched_kws = scores[best_category]\n\n    if best_score == 0:\n        return None, []\n\n    return best_category, matched_kws\n\n\ndef find_best_codebook_match(segment, codebook_entries, station):\n    \"\"\"Find the best matching codebook entry for a segment.\"\"\"\n    segment_fixed = fix_typos(segment)\n    segment_cleaned = clean_for_matching(segment_fixed)\n    segment_lower = segment_cleaned.lower()\n\n    detected_category, category_keywords = detect_category(segment)\n    extracted_components = extract_components(segment)\n    extracted_signals = extract_signals(segment)\n\n    best_match = None\n    best_score = 0\n    best_rationale_parts = []\n\n    for entry in codebook_entries:\n        score = 0\n        rationale_parts = []\n\n        # Check category alignment\n        entry_category = entry.get('category_lv2', '')\n        if detected_category and detected_category == entry_category:\n            score += 0.25\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"Category match: {detected_category}\")\n\n        # Check keywords from codebook\n        entry_keywords = entry.get('keywords_examples', '').lower().split(',')\n        keyword_matches = 0\n        for kw in entry_keywords:\n            kw = kw.strip()\n            if kw and kw in segment_lower:\n                keyword_matches += 1\n\n        if keyword_matches > 0:\n            score += min(keyword_matches * 0.12, 0.36)\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"Matched {keyword_matches} keywords\")\n\n        # Check station scope\n        station_scope = entry.get('station_scope', '')\n        if station and station in station_scope:\n            score += 0.1\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"Station {station} in scope\")\n\n        # Check component match in standard_label\n        standard_label = entry.get('standard_label', '')\n        for comp in extracted_components:\n            if comp.upper() in standard_label.upper():\n                score += 0.15\n                rationale_parts.append(f\"Component {comp} in label\")\n                break\n\n        # Check signal match\n        for sig in extracted_signals:\n            if sig.upper() in standard_label.upper():\n                score += 0.1\n                rationale_parts.append(f\"Signal {sig} in label\")\n                break\n\n        # Update best match\n        if score > best_score:\n            best_score = score\n            best_match = entry\n            best_rationale_parts = rationale_parts\n\n    return best_match, best_score, best_rationale_parts\n\n\ndef calculate_confidence(base_score, segment, match_entry):\n    \"\"\"Calculate calibrated confidence score.\"\"\"\n    # Base from matching score\n    confidence = min(base_score, 0.95)\n\n    # Adjust based on segment clarity\n    segment_len = len(segment)\n    if segment_len < 10:\n        confidence *= 0.85  # Short segments less reliable\n\n    # Check for hedging language\n    hedging = ['疑似', 'suspect', 'maybe', '可能', 'possible']\n    if any(h in segment.lower() for h in hedging):\n        confidence *= 0.92\n\n    return round(confidence, 2)\n\n\ndef normalize_record(record, codebooks):\n    \"\"\"Normalize a single test log record.\"\"\"\n    record_id = record['record_id']\n    product_id = record['product_id']\n    station = record['station']\n    engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n    raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n\n print(f\"Warning: No codebook for product {product_id}\")\n        return None\n\n    # Segment the raw text\n    segments = segment_raw_text(raw_reason_text)\n\n    normalized_segments = []\n    for idx, segment in enumerate(segments, start=1):\n        segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{idx}\"\n\n        # Find span_text (must be from raw_reason_text)\n        # Try to find this segment or a close match in the original text\n        span_text = segment\n        if segment not in raw_reason_text:\n            # Try to find a matching portion\n            for window_size in range(len(segment), 10, -5):\n                for start in range(len(segment) - window_size + 1):\n                    window = segment[start:start + window_size]\n                    if window in raw_reason_text:\n                        span_text = window\n                        break\n                if span_text in raw_reason_text:\n                    break\n            # Fallback: use a portion that exists\n            if span_text not in raw_reason_text:\n                # Find longest substring that exists\n                for length in range(len(segment), 5, -1):\n                    for start in \"\"\"Calculate calibrated confidence score.\"\"\"\n    # Base from matching score\n    confidence = min(base_score, 0.95)\n\n    # Adjust based on segment clarity\n    segment_len = len(segment)\n segment.lower() for h in hedging):\n        confidence *= 0.92\n\n    return round(confidence, 2)\n\n\ndef normalize_record(record, codebooks):\n    \"\"\"Normalize a single test log record.\"\"\"\n codebook_entries, station, product_id):\n    \"\"\"Find the best matching codebook entry for a segment.\"\"\"\n    segment_fixed = fix_typos(segment)\n    segment_cleaned = clean_for_matching(segment_fixed)\n    segment_lower = segment_cleaned.lower()\n\n    # Detect categories in this segment\n    detected_cats = detect_defect_categories(segment)\n    detected_cat_names best_rationale_parts = []\n\n    for entry in codebook_entries:\n        score = 0\n        rationale_parts = []\n\n        entry_category = entry.get('category_lv2', '')\n        entry_keywords = score.\"\"\"\n    if is_unknown:\n        # UNKNOWN predictions should have lower confidence\n        return round(min(base_score * 0.6 + 0.15, 0.45), 2)\n\n    # Scale base score to confidence range\n    # base_score typically 0.3-1.0, we want confidence 0.5-0.95\n    confidence = 0.5 + (base_score - 0.3) * (0.45 / 0.7)\n    confidence = max(0.5, min(confidence, 0.95))\n\n    # Adjust based on segment clarity\n    segment_len = len(segment)\n    if segment_len < 15:\n        confidence *= 0.95\n\n    # Hedging language penalty\n    hedging = ['疑似', 'suspect', 'maybe', '可能', 'possible', in range(len(segment), 5, -1):\n        for start in range(len(segment) - length + 1):\n            substr = segment[start:start + length]\n            if substr in raw_text and len(substr) > segment_raw_text(raw_text):\n    \"\"\"Split raw text into meaningful segments.\"\"\"\n    # Separator patterns for multi-defect descriptions\n    # Be careful: we want to split on logical separators but not break phrases\n\n    # First, handle the bilingual slash separators and semicolons\n    separators = r'(?:\\s*/\\s*|\\s*;\\s*|\\s*、\\s*|\\s*&\\s*|\\s*\\+\\s*)'\n\n    raw_segments = seen something very similar\n        is_dup = False\n        for seen in seen_normalized:\n            if len(normalized) > 5 and (normalized in seen or seen in normalized):\n                is_dup =  score += 1\n                matched_keywords.append(kw)\n        scores[category] = (score, matched_keywords)\n\n    # Get best category\n    best_category = max(scores.keys(), key=lambda k: scores[k][0])\n    best_score, matched_kws = scores[best_category]\n\n    if best_score == 0:\n        return None, []\n\n    return best_category, matched_kws\n\n\ndef find_best_codebook_match(segment, codebook_entries, station):\n    \"\"\"Find the best matching codebook entry for a segment.\"\"\"\n    segment_fixed = fix_typos(segment)\n    segment_cleaned = entry_category:\n            score += 0.25\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"Category match: {detected_category}\")\n\n        # Check keywords from codebook\n        entry_keywords = entry.get('keywords_examples', '').lower().split(',')\n        keyword_matches = 0\n        for kw in entry_keywords:\n            kw = kw.strip()\n            if kw and kw in segment_lower:\n                keyword_matches += 1\n\n        if keyword_matches > 0:\n            score += min(keyword_matches * 0.12, 0.36)\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"Matched {keyword_matches} keywords\")\n\n        # Check station scope\n        station_scope = entry.get('station_scope', '')\n        if station and station in station_scope:\n            score += 0.1\n            rationale_parts.append(f\"Station {station} in scope\")\n\n        # Check component match in standard_label\n        standard_label = entry.get('standard_label', '')\n        for comp in extracted_components:\n            if comp.upper() in standard_label.upper():\n                score += 0.15\n                rationale_parts.append(f\"Component {comp} in label\")\n                break\n\n        # Check signal match\n        for sig in extracted_signals:\n            if sig.upper() in standard_label.upper():\n                score += 0.1\n                rationale_parts.append(f\"Signal {sig} in label\")\n                break\n\n        # Update best match\n        if score > best_score:\n            best_score = score\n            best_match = entry\n            best_rationale_parts = rationale_parts\n\n    return best_match, best_score, best_rationale_parts\n\n\ndef calculate_confidence(base_score, segment, match_entry):\n    \"\"\"Calculate calibrated confidence score.\"\"\"\n    # Base from matching score\n    confidence = min(base_score, 0.95)\n\n    # Adjust based on segment clarity\n    segment_len = len(segment)\n    if segment_len < 10:\n        confidence *= 0.85  # Short segments less reliable\n\n    # Check for hedging language\n    hedging = ['疑似', 'suspect', 'maybe', '可能', 'possible']\n    if any(h in segment.lower() for h in hedging):\n        confidence *= 0.92\n\n    return round(confidence, 2)\n\n\ndef normalize_record(record, codebooks):\n    \"\"\"Normalize a single test log record.\"\"\"\n    record_id = record['record_id']\n    product_id = record['product_id']\n    station = record['station']\n    engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n    raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n\n idx, segment in enumerate(segments, start=1):\n        segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{idx}\"\n\n        # Find span_text (must be from raw_reason_text)\n        # Try to find this segment or a close match in the original text\n        span_text = segment\n        if segment not in raw_reason_text:\n            # Try to find a matching portion\n            for window_size in range(len(segment), 10, -5):\n                for start in range(len(segment) - window_size + 1):\n                    window = segment[start:start + window_size]\n                    if window in raw_reason_text:\n                        span_text = window\n                        break\n                if span_text in raw_reason_text:\n                    break\n            # Fallback: use a portion that exists\n            if span_text not in raw_reason_text:\n                # Find longest substring that exists\n                for length in range(len(segment), 5, -1):\n                    for start in range(len(segment) - length + 1):\n                        substr = segment[start:start + length]\n                        if substr in raw_reason_text:\n                            span_text = substr\n                            break\n                    if span_text in raw_reason_text:\n                        break\n                else:\n                    span_text = segment[:min(50, score >= 0.3:\n            confidence = calculate_confidence(score, segment, best_match)\n\n            # If confidence too low, mark as UNKNOWN\n            if confidence < 0.5:\n                normalized_segments.append({\n                    \"segment_id\": segment_id,\n                    \"span_text\": span_text,\n                    \"pred_code\": \"UNKNOWN\",\n                    \"pred_label\": \"\",\n                    \"confidence\": round(confidence * 0.8, 2),  # Lower for UNKNOWN\n                    \"rationale\": f\"Low confidence ({confidence:.2f}): \" + \"; \".join(rationale_parts) if rationale_parts else \"No strong match found\"\n                })\n            else:\n                normalized_segments.append({\n                    \"segment_id\":     \"pred_label\": \"\",\n                \"confidence\": round(score * 0.5 + 0.15, 2) if score > 0 else 0.2,\n                \"rationale\": f\"No matching codebook entry found for segment\"\n            })\n\n    return {\n        \"record_id\": record_id,\n        \"product_id\": product_id,\n        \"station\": station,\n        \"engineer_id\": engineer_id,\n        \"raw_reason_text\":
